SQL Query: CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS notification_views (
          id SERIAL PRIMARY KEY,
          notification_id INTEGER NOT NULL,
          user_id INTEGER NOT NULL,

          id SERIAL PRIMARY KEY,
          notification_id INTEGER NOT NULL,

          id SERIAL PRIMARY KEY,
          notification_id INTEGER NOT NULL,
          user_id INTEGER NOT NULL,

        )

[2026-02-01T08:18:44.360Z] - CUSTOM QUERY - Created notification_views table
SQL Query: CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS notification_views (
          id SERIAL PRIMARY KEY,
          notification_id INTEGER NOT NULL,
          user_id INTEGER NOT NULL,
          viewed_at TIMESTAMP DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
          FOREIGN KEY (notification_id) REFERENCES notifications(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
          FOREIGN KEY (user_id) REFERENCES users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
          UNIQUE(notification_id, user_id)
        )

[2026-02-03T14:49:42.182Z] - CUSTOM QUERY - Created notification_views table
SQL Query: CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS notification_views (
          id SERIAL PRIMARY KEY,
          notification_id INTEGER NOT NULL,
          user_id INTEGER NOT NULL,
          viewed_at TIMESTAMP DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
          FOREIGN KEY (notification_id) REFERENCES notifications(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
          FOREIGN KEY (user_id) REFERENCES users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
          UNIQUE(notification_id, user_id)
        )

             ADD CONSTRAINT bus_details_ownership_type_check CHECK (ownership_type IN ('owned', 'leased'));
           END IF;
         END $$;
---
